# utils

## copyAgentChat

Copies a chat mapping for synchronous descriptor callers.

**Throws**

TypeError for a noncanonical mapping.

**Example**

```ts
import { copyAgentChat } from "@relkit/agents";

const chat = copyAgentChat({ input: "message", output: "answer" });
void chat;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAgentChat: (value: unknown) => AgentChatMapping | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client-chat.ts#L63)

## copyAgentChatEffect

Validates the fixed chat input and output mapping.

**Example**

```ts
import { Effect } from "effect";
import { copyAgentChatEffect } from "@relkit/agents";

const chat = Effect.runSync(copyAgentChatEffect({ input: "message", output: "answer" }));
void chat;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAgentChatEffect: (
  value: unknown,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Readonly<{ input: "message"; output: "answer" }> | undefined,
  AgentClientPolicyError,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client-chat.ts#L24)

# utils

## copyAgentControls

Copies client controls for synchronous descriptor callers.

**Throws**

TypeError when an unsupported or duplicate control is present.

**Example**

```ts
import { copyAgentControls } from "@relkit/agents";

const controls = copyAgentControls(["stop"]);
void controls;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAgentControls: (value: unknown) => readonly AgentControl[] | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client-controls.ts#L75)

## copyAgentControlsEffect

Validates and freezes client control capabilities.

**Example**

```ts
import { Effect } from "effect";
import { copyAgentControlsEffect } from "@relkit/agents";

const controls = Effect.runSync(copyAgentControlsEffect(["stop"]));
void controls;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAgentControlsEffect: (
  value: unknown,
) => Effect.Effect<ReadonlyArray<AgentControl> | undefined, AgentClientPolicyError, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client-controls.ts#L26)

# utils

## copyAgentClientPolicy

Copies a client policy for synchronous descriptor callers.

**Throws**

TypeError for malformed access, state, or event declarations.

**Example**

```ts
import { copyAgentClientPolicy } from "@relkit/agents";

const policy = copyAgentClientPolicy({ public: true }, new Set<string>());
void policy;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAgentClientPolicy: <StateKey extends string>(
  value: unknown,
  allowedStateKeys?: ReadonlySet<StateKey>,
) => AgentClientPolicy<(...args: any[]) => unknown, StateKey> | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client-policy.ts#L67)

## copyAgentClientPolicyEffect

Validates and freezes client access, state keys, and event schemas.

**Example**

```ts
import { Effect } from "effect";
import { copyAgentClientPolicyEffect } from "@relkit/agents";

const policy = Effect.runSync(copyAgentClientPolicyEffect({ public: true }, new Set<string>()));
void policy;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAgentClientPolicyEffect: <StateKey extends string>(
  value: unknown,
  allowedStateKeys?: ReadonlySet<StateKey> | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<
  | Readonly<{
      events?: Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>>;
      state?: ReadonlyArray<StateKey>;
      public: true;
    }>
  | Readonly<{
      events?: Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>>;
      state?: ReadonlyArray<StateKey>;
      authorize: (...args: any[]) => unknown;
    }>
  | undefined,
  AgentClientPolicyError,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client-policy.ts#L26)

# utils

## AgentChatMapping (interface)

Fixed input and output field mapping for an agent chat client.

**Example**

```ts
const chat: AgentChatMapping = { input: "message", output: "answer" };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentChatMapping {
  readonly input: "message";
  readonly output: "answer";
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client.types.ts#L43)

## AgentClientEvents (type alias)

Named public events and their Standard Schema validators.
Names must be nonempty before the policy is published.

**Example**

```ts
import { z } from "@relkit/schema";
const events: AgentClientEvents = { completed: z.string() };
```

**Signature**

```ts
type AgentClientEvents = Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1>>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client.types.ts#L19)

## AgentClientPolicy (type alias)

Client visibility, selected state, and public event schemas.
Exactly one of public access and an authorization guard must be supplied.

**Example**

```ts
const policy: AgentClientPolicy = { public: true, state: ["answer"] };
```

**Signature**

```ts
type AgentClientPolicy<
  Guard = (...args: any[]) => unknown,
  StateKey extends string = string,
  Events extends AgentClientEvents = AgentClientEvents,
> = (
  | { readonly public: true; readonly authorize?: never }
  | { readonly authorize: Guard; readonly public?: never }
) & {
  readonly state?: readonly StateKey[];
  readonly events?: Events;
};
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client.types.ts#L28)

## AgentControl (type alias)

Durable control capabilities that may be exposed to an agent client.

**Example**

```ts
const control: AgentControl = "stop";
```

**Signature**

```ts
type AgentControl = "steer" | "follow-up" | "stop" | "approve";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-client.types.ts#L9)

## AgentTelemetryService (interface)

Replaceable observer used by Effect operations and deterministic tests.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentTelemetryService {
  /** Records an operation without changing its Effect channels.
   * @param operation - Fixed agent operation name.
   * @param effect - Work to instrument.
   * @returns The same success, error, and dependency channels.
   * @example telemetry.observe("capture.policy", Effect.succeed({ mode: "off" }));
   */
  readonly observe: <A, E, R>(
    operation: AgentOperation,
    effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,
  ) => Effect.Effect<A, E, R>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/agent-telemetry.types.ts#L176)

Throws for an invalid schema in existing synchronous authoring calls.

**Throws**

TypeError when the value is not a Standard Schema v1 validator.


# Approvals

## approveApproval

Moves a pending approval to the approved state.

**Throws**

ApprovalStateError for a malformed or final decision.

**Example**

```ts
import { approveApproval, createApproval } from "@relkit/app/agents";

const pending = createApproval({
  invocationId: "run",
  toolCallId: "call",
  toolId: "send",
  sideEffect: "write",
  policy: "always",
});
const approved = approveApproval(pending);
void approved;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const approveApproval: (approval: ApprovalRecord) => ApprovedApproval;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/approval.ts#L99)

Since v0.4.0

## denyApproval

Moves a pending approval to the denied state.

**Throws**

ApprovalStateError for a malformed or final decision.

**Example**

```ts
import { createApproval, denyApproval } from "@relkit/app/agents";

const pending = createApproval({
  invocationId: "run",
  toolCallId: "call",
  toolId: "send",
  sideEffect: "write",
  policy: "always",
});
const denied = denyApproval(pending);
void denied;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const denyApproval: (approval: ApprovalRecord) => DeniedApproval;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/approval.ts#L132)

Since v0.4.0

```ts
}
```

# utils

## AgentCapturePolicy (interface)

Controls whether an agent may retain redacted content in its spans.
Development capture requires a positive byte limit; off ignores the other fields.

**Example**

```ts
const policy: AgentCapturePolicy = { mode: "development-redacted", maxBytes: 1024 };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentCapturePolicy {
  readonly mode: "off" | "development-redacted";
  readonly maxBytes?: number;
  readonly redactKeys?: readonly string[];
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/capture.types.ts#L10)

## AgentCaptureRecord (interface)

Captured JSON and its encoded size, or a marker when capture was truncated.
The content property is absent whenever truncated is true.

**Example**

```ts
const record: AgentCaptureRecord = { mode: "development-redacted", bytes: 0, truncated: true };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentCaptureRecord {
  readonly mode: "development-redacted";
  readonly bytes: number;
  readonly truncated: boolean;
  readonly content?: JsonValue;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/capture.types.ts#L23)

## AgentSpanCapture (interface)

Optional input and output captures attached to one agent span.
Absent fields were disabled or unavailable at capture time.

**Example**

```ts
const capture: AgentSpanCapture = {
  input: { mode: "development-redacted", bytes: 0, truncated: true },
};
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentSpanCapture {
  readonly input?: AgentCaptureRecord;
  readonly output?: AgentCaptureRecord;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/capture.types.ts#L39)

## "./client-contract-metadata.types.js" (namespace export)

Re-exports all named exports from the "./client-contract-metadata.types.js" module.

**Signature**

```ts
export * from "./client-contract-metadata.types.js";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.ts#L21)

## agentClientContractMetadata

Builds native agent metadata for existing synchronous callers.

**Throws**

The original invalid metadata error.

**Example**

```ts
agentClientContractMetadata(options);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const agentClientContractMetadata: (
  options: AgentClientMetadataOptions,
) => AgentClientContractMetadata;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.ts#L39)

## agentClientContractMetadataEffect

Builds client contract metadata for a native agent.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(agentClientContractMetadataEffect(options));
```

**Signature**

```ts

  options: AgentClientMetadataOptions,

```

# utils

## AgentClientContractMetadata (interface)

Complete metadata needed to generate an agent client contract.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentClientContractMetadata {
  readonly tools: readonly ClientToolMetadata[];
  readonly state: readonly ClientTypeField[];
  readonly events: readonly ClientEventMetadata[];
  readonly scopes: readonly ClientScopeMetadata[];
  readonly waiting: readonly ClientWaitingMetadata[];
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L27)

## AgentClientMetadataOptions (interface)

Inputs for native agent client metadata.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentClientMetadataOptions {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly tools: readonly AgentTool[];
  readonly middleware: readonly AgentMiddleware[];
  readonly client?: AgentClientPolicy;
  readonly subagents?: readonly SubagentMetadataSource[];
  readonly interruptOn?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L42)

## ClientEventMetadata (type alias)

Client visible event schema or dynamic event marker.

**Signature**

```ts
type ClientEventMetadata = ClientTypeField | { readonly kind: "dynamic" };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L7)

## ClientScopeMetadata (interface)

Client visible agent, subagent, or graph node scope.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ClientScopeMetadata {
  readonly kind: "agent" | "subagent" | "node" | "dynamic";
  readonly id?: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L15)

## ClientToolMetadata (type alias)

Client visible tool schema or dynamic tool marker.

**Signature**

```ts
type ClientToolMetadata =
  | {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly input: ClientSchemaMetadata;
      readonly output: ClientSchemaMetadata;
    }
  | { readonly kind: "dynamic" };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L10)

## ClientWaitingMetadata (interface)

Client visible continuation response schema.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ClientWaitingMetadata {
  readonly scope: ClientScopeMetadata;
  readonly response: ClientSchemaMetadata;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L21)

## GraphClientMetadataOptions (interface)

Inputs for graph client metadata.

**Signature**

```ts

  readonly id: string;

}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/client-contract-metadata.types.ts#L52)


## createDeepAgentBucketBackendEffect

Builds a DeepAgents backend from a RELKIT bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(createDeepAgentBucketBackendEffect(bucket));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createDeepAgentBucketBackendEffect: (
  bucket: DeepAgentBucketClient,
  options?: DeepAgentBucketBackendOptions | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Readonly<{
    ls: (path: string) => Promise<LsResult>;
    read: (path: string, offset?: number, limit?: number) => Promise<ReadResult>;
    readRaw: (path: string) => Promise<ReadRawResult>;
    write: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<WriteResult>;
    edit: (
      path: string,
      oldText: string,
      newText: string,
      replaceAll?: boolean,
    ) => Promise<EditResult>;
    grep: (
      pattern: string,
      path?: string | null,
      glob?: string | null,
      maxCount?: number | null,
    ) => Promise<GrepResult>;
    glob: (pattern: string, path?: string) => Promise<GlobResult>;
    delete: (path: string) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
    uploadFiles: (files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>) => Promise<Array<FileUploadResponse>>;
    downloadFiles: (paths: string[]) => Promise<Array<FileDownloadResponse>>;
  }>,
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-backend.ts#L27)

# utils

## removeBucketPath

Removes a bucket path for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await removeBucketPath(context, "/notes");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const removeBucketPath: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  path: string,
) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-delete.ts#L57)

## removeBucketPathEffect

Removes a virtual file or directory with at most eight bucket requests in flight.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(removeBucketPathEffect("/notes"), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const removeBucketPathEffect: (
  path: string,
) => Effect.Effect<
  | { error: string; path?: never; filesUpdate?: never }
  | { path: string; filesUpdate: null; error?: never },
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-delete.ts#L19)

## `deepagent-bucket-files.types.ts`

---

## Exports Grouped by Category

- [utils](#utils)
  - [DeepAgentBucketClient (interface)](#deepagentbucketclient-interface)

---

# utils

## DeepAgentBucketClient (interface)

IO operations required by the DeepAgents bucket bridge.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DeepAgentBucketClient {
  readonly put: (
    key: string,
    bytes: Uint8Array,
    options?: {
      readonly contentType?: string;
      readonly metadata?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
    },
  ) => Promise<void>;
  readonly get: (key: string) => Promise<Uint8Array | undefined>;
  readonly head: (key: string) => Promise<DeepAgentBucketMetadata | undefined>;
  readonly delete: (key: string) => Promise<void>;
  readonly exists: (key: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  readonly list: (prefix?: string) => Promise<readonly string[]>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-files.types.ts#L20)

## DeepAgentBucketContext (interface)

Bucket client and prefix bound to one DeepAgents backend.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DeepAgentBucketContext {
  readonly bucket: DeepAgentBucketClient;
  readonly prefix: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-files.types.ts#L4)

## DeepAgentBucketFileData (type alias)

DeepAgents file payload that carries a concrete MIME type.

**Signature**

```ts
type DeepAgentBucketFileData = Extract<FileData, { readonly mimeType: string }>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-files.types.ts#L17)

## DeepAgentBucketMetadata (interface)

Metadata needed to expose a bucket object as a virtual file.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DeepAgentBucketMetadata {
  readonly contentType?: string;
  readonly size?: number;
  readonly metadata?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-files.types.ts#L10)

```ts
declare const grepFiles: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  pattern: string,
  path?: string | null,
  globPattern?: string | null,
  maxCount?: number | null,
) => Promise<GrepResult>;
```

## grepFilesEffect

Searches text files in key order and stops as soon as the match cap is exceeded.

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(grepFilesEffect("TODO"), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const grepFilesEffect: (
  pattern: string,
  path?: string | null | undefined,
  globPattern?: string | null | undefined,
  maxCount?: number | null | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<
  | { matches: Array<GrepMatch>; truncated: boolean }
  | { matches: Array<GrepMatch>; truncated?: never },
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

## createThreadBucketBackend

Creates a private thread backend for existing synchronous callers.

**Throws**

The original invalid bucket or thread error.

**Example**

```ts
const backend = createThreadBucketBackend(bucket, "agent", "thread");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createThreadBucketBackend: (
  bucket: DeepAgentBucketClient,
  agentId: string,
  threadId: string,
) => Readonly<{
  ls: (path: string) => Promise<LsResult>;
  read: (path: string, offset?: number, limit?: number) => Promise<ReadResult>;
  readRaw: (path: string) => Promise<ReadRawResult>;
  write: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<WriteResult>;
  edit: (
    path: string,
    oldText: string,
    newText: string,
    replaceAll?: boolean,
  ) => Promise<EditResult>;
  grep: (
    pattern: string,
    path?: string | null,
    glob?: string | null,
    maxCount?: number | null,
  ) => Promise<GrepResult>;
  glob: (pattern: string, path?: string) => Promise<GlobResult>;
  delete: (path: string) => Promise<DeleteResult>;
  uploadFiles: (files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>) => Promise<Array<FileUploadResponse>>;
  downloadFiles: (paths: string[]) => Promise<Array<FileDownloadResponse>>;
}>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-scope.ts#L39)


```ts
declare const createThreadBucketBackendEffect: (

  Readonly<{

      path: string,
```

# utils

## downloadBucketFiles

Downloads files for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await downloadBucketFiles(context, ["/a"]);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const downloadBucketFiles: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  paths: string[],
) => Promise<FileDownloadResponse[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-transfer.ts#L93)

## downloadBucketFilesEffect

Downloads files in input order with a result for each path.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(downloadBucketFilesEffect(["/a"]), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const downloadBucketFilesEffect: (
  paths: Array<string>,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Array<
    | { path: string; content: null; error: "file_not_found" }
    | { path: string; content: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>; error: null }
    | { path: string; content: null; error: "invalid_path" }
  >,
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-transfer.ts#L62)

## uploadBucketFiles

Uploads files for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await uploadBucketFiles(context, [["/a", bytes]]);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const uploadBucketFiles: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  files: Array<[string, Uint8Array]>,
) => Promise<FileUploadResponse[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-transfer.ts#L47)

## uploadBucketFilesEffect

Uploads files in input order so repeated paths retain last-write behavior.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(
  Effect.provide(uploadBucketFilesEffect([["/a", bytes]]), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const uploadBucketFilesEffect: (
  files: Array<[string, Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>]>,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Array<{ path: string; error: null } | { path: string; error: "invalid_path" }>,
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-transfer.ts#L15)

# utils

## editBucket

Edits text for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await editBucket(context, "/a.md", "old", "new", true);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const editBucket: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  path: string,
  oldText: string,
  newText: string,
  replaceAll?: boolean,
) => Promise<EditResult>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-write.ts#L93)

## editBucketEffect

Replaces text with the existing DeepAgents edit rules.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(
  Effect.provide(editBucketEffect("/a.md", "old", "new"), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const editBucketEffect: (
  path: string,
  oldText: string,
  newText: string,
  replaceAll?: any,
) => Effect.Effect<
  | { error: string; path?: never; filesUpdate?: never; occurrences?: never }
  | { path: string; filesUpdate: null; occurrences: number; error?: never },
  DeepAgentBucketFailure,
  DeepAgentBucket
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-write.ts#L59)

## writeBucket

Writes text for existing DeepAgents Promise callers.

**Throws**

A cancellation or timeout failure from the bucket client.

**Example**

```ts
await writeBucket(context, "/a.md", "hi");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const writeBucket: (
  context: DeepAgentBucketContext,
  path: string,
  content: string,
) => Promise<WriteResult>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-write.ts#L40)

## writeBucketEffect

Writes a text file through the current bucket service.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(Effect.provide(writeBucketEffect("/a.md", "hi"), deepAgentBucketLayer(context)));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const writeBucketEffect: (
  path: string,
  content: string,
) => Effect.Effect<{ path: string; filesUpdate: null }, DeepAgentBucketFailure, DeepAgentBucket>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/deepagent-bucket-write.ts#L16)

## `define-agent-build.ts`

## Exports Grouped by Category

- [utils](#utils)
  - [prepareAgentDescriptor](#prepareagentdescriptor)

---

# utils

## prepareAgentDescriptor

Validates agent options and returns an assembler for a resolved identity.

**Throws**

Validation errors from fields and client policy.

**Example**

```ts
prepareAgentDescriptor(options)("support");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const prepareAgentDescriptor: <
  const Id extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Middleware extends readonly AgentMiddleware[],
  const Tools extends readonly AgentTool[],
>(
  options: DefineAgentOptions<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, Middleware, Tools>,
) => (
  id: Id,
) => AgentDescriptor<
  Id,
  InferInput<InputSchema>,
  InferOutput<OutputSchema>,
  InputSchema,
  OutputSchema,
  Middleware,
  Tools
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/define-agent-build.ts#L25)



**Signature**

```ts
  value: DeepAgentCapabilities,

```

```ts
}
```

## AgentMiddlewareState (type alias)

Public state composed from middleware state schemas.

**Signature**

```ts
type AgentMiddlewareState<Middleware extends readonly AgentMiddleware[]> =
  Middleware extends readonly [infer First, ...infer Rest]
    ? First extends AgentMiddleware
      ? Rest extends readonly AgentMiddleware[]
        ? MiddlewareState<First> & AgentMiddlewareState<Rest>
        : MiddlewareState<First>
      : {}
    : {};
```

# Agents

## defineAgent

Defines a native agent for existing synchronous authoring callers.

**Throws**

The original invalid authoring or identity error.

**Example**

```ts
import { defineAgent } from "@relkit/app/agents";
import { z } from "@relkit/app/schema";

const support = defineAgent({
  id: "support",
  input: z.object({ message: z.string() }),
  output: z.object({ answer: z.string() }),
  instructions: "Reply briefly.",
  tools: [],
  limits: { maxSteps: 4, maxToolCalls: 2, timeoutMs: 30_000 },
});
void support;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineAgent: <
  const Id extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Middleware extends readonly AgentMiddleware[] = readonly [],
  const Tools extends readonly AgentTool[] = ReadonlyArray<AgentTool>,
>(
  options: DefineAgentOptions<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, Middleware, Tools>,
) => AgentDescriptor<
  Id,
  InferInput<InputSchema>,
  InferOutput<OutputSchema>,
  InputSchema,
  OutputSchema,
  Middleware,
  Tools
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/define-agent.ts#L91)

Since v0.4.0

```ts
      string,

) => Effect.Effect<GraphNodeResult<OutputSchema, Ends>, GraphNodeValidationFailure, never>;
```

```ts
export interface GraphNodeDescriptor<

  ResumeSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = StandardSchemaV1 | undefined,

> extends DescriptorMetadata {
```

```ts
type GraphNodeHandler<
```

```ts
type GraphNodeImplementation<
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,

  Ends extends readonly string[],
> = (
  input: InferOutput<InputSchema>,
  config?: LangGraphRunnableConfig,
) => MaybePromise<
  | NodeUpdateInput<OutputSchema>
  | Command<unknown, NodeUpdateInput<OutputSchema>, CommandNodes<Ends>>
>;
```

```ts

  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,

>(
```

```ts
>(
```

# utils

## DefineGraphOptions (interface)

Authoring options for a native LangGraph agent descriptor.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DefineGraphOptions<
  Id extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  State extends AnyStateSchema,
  Nodes extends readonly GraphNodeLike[],
> extends DescriptorMetadata {
  readonly id?: Id;
  readonly state: State;
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly output: OutputSchema;
  readonly nodes: Nodes & UniqueNodes<Nodes>;
  readonly edges: (
    graph: GraphEdgeBuilder<NoInfer<Extract<GraphNodeId<Nodes>, string>>, GraphState<State>>,
  ) => unknown;
  readonly limits: AgentLimits;
  readonly stateProfile?: string;
  readonly client?: AgentClientPolicy<(...args: any[]) => unknown, GraphStateKey<State>>;
  readonly controls?: readonly AgentControl[];
  readonly checkpointer?: BaseCheckpointSaver | CheckpointerResource;
  readonly store?: BaseStore | MemoryResource;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/define-graph.types.ts#L72)

## GraphDescriptor (interface)

Inspectable agent descriptor backed by a native LangGraph graph.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphDescriptor<
  Id extends string = string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
  State extends AnyStateSchema = AnyStateSchema,
  Nodes extends readonly GraphNodeLike[] = readonly GraphNodeLike[],
>
  extends DescriptorBase<"agent", Id>, AgentRef<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema> {
  readonly execution: "graph";
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly output: OutputSchema;
  readonly nodes: Nodes;
  readonly workflow: GraphWorkflow;
  readonly instructions: "";
  readonly tools: readonly [];
  readonly middleware: readonly [];
  readonly limits: AgentLimits;
  readonly stateProfile?: string;
  readonly client?: AgentClientPolicy<(...args: any[]) => unknown, GraphStateKey<State>>;
  readonly controls?: readonly AgentControl[];
  readonly clientContract: AgentClientContractMetadata;
  readonly [GRAPH_EXECUTION]: GraphExecution<GraphState<State>>;
  readonly asGraphNode: <const NodeId extends string = Id>(
    options?: GraphAsNodeOptions<NodeId>,
  ) => SubgraphNodeDescriptor<NodeId, InputSchema, OutputSchema>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/define-graph.types.ts#L45)

## GraphExecution (interface)

Runtime state and persistence declarations hidden on a graph descriptor.

```ts
export interface GraphExecution<State = any> {
  readonly state: AnyStateSchema;

  readonly checkpointer?: BaseCheckpointSaver | CheckpointerResource;
  readonly store?: BaseStore | MemoryResource;
}
```

## MemoryResource (type alias)

Memory store resource passed to a LangGraph runtime.

**Signature**

```ts

```

## PersistenceContext (interface)

Environment made available while acquiring persistence.

**Signature**

```ts

  readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/define-persistence.types.ts#L6)

## PersistenceOptions (interface)

Acquisition and disposal configuration for an agent persistence resource.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface PersistenceOptions<Value> {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly client: Value | ((context: PersistenceContext) => MaybePromise<Value>);
  readonly ownership?: "owned" | "borrowed";
  readonly dispose?: (value: Value) => MaybePromise<void>;
}
```

## PersistenceResource (interface)

Lazily acquired, explicitly released persistence descriptor.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface PersistenceResource<Kind extends "checkpointer" | "memory", Value> {
  readonly [RELKIT_PERSISTENCE]: true;
  readonly kind: "agent-persistence";
  readonly resource: Kind;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly ownership: "owned" | "borrowed";
  readonly acquire: (context: PersistenceContext) => Promise<Value>;
  readonly release: () => Promise<void>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/define-persistence.types.ts#L11)

# utils

## GraphConditionalRoute (type alias)

Label based graph conditional route callback.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphConditionalRoute<Id extends string, State, Key extends string> = (
  state: State,
  config: LangGraphRunnableConfig,
) => MaybePromise<Key | Send<Id, Partial<State>> | readonly (Key | Send<Id, Partial<State>>)[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L11)

## GraphEdgeBuilder (interface)

Mutable graph edge authoring interface backed by Effect operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphEdgeBuilder<Id extends string, State> {
  /** Adds a static or join edge.
   * @param start - One or more source nodes.
   * @param end - Destination node.
   * @returns This builder.
   * @throws TypeError for invalid or duplicate edges.
   * @example edge.addEdge("start", "finish");
   */
  addEdge(
    start: typeof START | NoInfer<Id> | readonly NoInfer<Id>[],
    end: NoInfer<Id> | typeof END,
  ): GraphEdgeBuilder<Id, State>;
  /** Adds a dynamic edge.
   * @param source - Source node.
   * @param route - Route callback.
   * @returns This builder.
   * @throws TypeError for invalid or duplicate routes.
   * @example edge.addConditionalEdges("start", () => "finish");
   */
  addConditionalEdges(
    source: typeof START | NoInfer<Id>,
    route: GraphRoute<NoInfer<Id>, State>,
  ): GraphEdgeBuilder<Id, State>;
  /** Adds a labeled dynamic edge.
   * @param source - Source node.
   * @param route - Route callback.
   * @param destinations - Label to destination mapping.
   * @returns This builder.
   * @throws TypeError for invalid or duplicate routes.
   * @example edge.addConditionalEdges("start", () => "yes", { yes: "finish" });
   */
  addConditionalEdges<const Key extends string>(
    source: typeof START | NoInfer<Id>,
    route: GraphConditionalRoute<NoInfer<Id>, State, Key>,
    destinations: Readonly<Record<Key, NoInfer<Id> | typeof END>>,
  ): GraphEdgeBuilder<Id, State>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L17)

## GraphEdgeOperation (type alias)

Recorded edge operation consumed by the LangGraph compiler.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphEdgeOperation<Id extends string, State> =
  | {
      readonly kind: "edge";
      readonly start: typeof START | Id | readonly Id[];
      readonly end: Id | typeof END;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: "conditional";
      readonly source: typeof START | Id;
      readonly route: (state: State, config: LangGraphRunnableConfig) => MaybePromise<unknown>;
      readonly destinations?: Readonly<Record<string, Id | typeof END>>;
    };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L50)

## GraphRoute (type alias)

Native graph conditional route callback.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphRoute<Id extends string, State> = (
  state: State,
  config: LangGraphRunnableConfig,
) => MaybePromise<Id | typeof END | Send<Id> | readonly (Id | Send<Id>)[]>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-edges.types.ts#L5)

## "./graph-subgraph.types.js" (namespace export)

Re-exports all named exports from the "./graph-subgraph.types.js" module.

**Signature**

```ts

```

## createSubgraphNode

Creates an embedded graph node for existing synchronous callers.

**Throws**

The original invalid options or destination error.

**Example**

```ts
const node = createSubgraphNode(graph, { id: "nested" });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createSubgraphNode: <
  const Id extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
>(
  graph: GraphDescriptor<string, InputSchema, OutputSchema>,
  options?: GraphAsNodeOptions<Id>,
) => SubgraphNodeDescriptor<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-subgraph.ts#L58)

## createSubgraphNodeEffect

Creates a native node backed by another graph.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(createSubgraphNodeEffect(graph, { id: "nested" }));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createSubgraphNodeEffect: <
  const Id extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
>(
  graph: GraphDescriptor<
    string,
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    AnyStateSchema,
    ReadonlyArray<GraphNodeLike>
  >,
  options?: GraphAsNodeOptions<Id> | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<
  SubgraphNodeDescriptor<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema>,
  GraphNodeValidationFailure,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-subgraph.ts#L20)

## isSubgraphNode

Checks embedded graph nodes for existing synchronous callers.

**Example**

```ts
if (isSubgraphNode(value)) subgraphForNode(value);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const isSubgraphNode: (value: unknown) => value is SubgraphNodeDescriptor;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-subgraph.ts#L92)

## isSubgraphNodeEffect

Checks whether a value is an embedded graph node.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(isSubgraphNodeEffect(value));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const isSubgraphNodeEffect: (value: unknown) => Effect.Effect<boolean, never, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-subgraph.ts#L76)

## subgraphForNode

Reads the source graph for existing synchronous callers.

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-subgraph.ts#L111)

```ts
  never,
```

## GraphAsNodeOptions (interface)

Authoring options for a graph embedded as a node.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphAsNodeOptions<Id extends string = string> {
  readonly id?: Id;
  readonly ends?: readonly string[];
}
```

## SubgraphNodeDescriptor (interface)

Native graph node whose action is another graph.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface SubgraphNodeDescriptor<
  Id extends string = string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
> {
  readonly [RELKIT_SUBGRAPH_NODE]: true;
  readonly kind: "subgraph-node";
  readonly id: Id;
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly output: OutputSchema;
  readonly ends: readonly string[];
  readonly [RELKIT_SUBGRAPH]: GraphDescriptor;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-subgraph.types.ts#L12)

## graphWorkflow

Projects a workflow for existing synchronous graph callers.

**Throws**

The original node or schema projection error.

**Example**

```ts
graphWorkflow(nodes, edges);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const graphWorkflow: <Id extends string, State>(
  nodes: readonly (GraphNodeAny | FunctionGraphNodeDescriptor | SubgraphNodeDescriptor)[],
  edges: readonly GraphEdgeOperation<Id, State>[],
) => GraphWorkflow;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-workflow.ts#L45)

## graphWorkflowEffect

Projects a frozen serializable graph workflow from authoring nodes and edges.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(graphWorkflowEffect(nodes, edges));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const graphWorkflowEffect: <Id extends string, State>(
  nodes: ReadonlyArray<
    | GraphNodeAny
    | FunctionGraphNodeDescriptor<
        string,
        string,
        unknown,
        unknown,
        ReadonlyArray<ErrorDescriptorAny>,
        StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>,
        StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>,
        FunctionDependencies
      >
    | SubgraphNodeDescriptor<
        string,
        StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>,
        StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>
      >
  >,
  edges: ReadonlyArray<GraphEdgeOperation<Id, State>>,
) => Effect.Effect<GraphWorkflow, GraphWorkflowFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-workflow.ts#L21)

# utils

## GraphWorkflow (interface)

Immutable serializable graph topology for runtime and clients.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphWorkflow {
  readonly version: 1;
  readonly start: typeof START;
  readonly end: typeof END;
  readonly nodes: readonly GraphWorkflowNode[];
  readonly edges: readonly GraphWorkflowEdge[];
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-workflow.types.ts#L28)

## GraphWorkflowEdge (type alias)

Serializable static, join, or conditional graph edge.

**Signature**

```ts
type GraphWorkflowEdge =
  | { readonly kind: "edge"; readonly from: string; readonly to: string }
  | { readonly kind: "join"; readonly from: readonly string[]; readonly to: string }
  | {
      readonly kind: "conditional";
      readonly from: string;
      readonly routes: readonly { readonly label: string; readonly to: string }[];
      readonly dynamic: boolean;
    };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-workflow.types.ts#L17)

## GraphWorkflowNode (interface)

Serializable node entry in an agent graph workflow.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface GraphWorkflowNode {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: "node" | "function" | "subgraph";
  readonly input: JsonValue;
  readonly output: JsonValue;
  readonly resume?: JsonValue;
  readonly ends: readonly string[];
  readonly targetFunctionId?: string;
  readonly workflow?: GraphWorkflow;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/graph-workflow.types.ts#L5)

# utils

## AgentEdgeRelationship (type alias)

Relationships reported by an agent's runtime hooks.

**Example**

```ts
const relationship: AgentEdgeRelationship = "uses-tool";
```

**Signature**

```ts
type AgentEdgeRelationship =
  | "targets-function"
  | "calls-function"
  | "enqueues-job"
  | "publishes-event"
  | "listens-to-event"
  | "uses-bucket"
  | "uses-cache"
  | "invokes-agent"
  | "exposes-as-tool"
  | "uses-tool"
  | "uses-provider-profile";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/observability.types.ts#L7)

## AgentObservabilitySink (interface)

Optional external sink for observed agent relationships.

**Example**

```ts
const sink: AgentObservabilitySink = { emit: (event) => send(event) };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentObservabilitySink {
  /** Accepts a public relationship event.
   * @param event - Versioned observed edge.
   * @returns Completion of best-effort sink delivery.
   * @example sink.emit({ protocol: "relkit.observability.hooks", version: 1, type: "edge.observed", edge });
   */
  readonly emit: (event: {
    readonly protocol: typeof AGENT_OBSERVABILITY_PROTOCOL;
    readonly version: typeof AGENT_OBSERVABILITY_VERSION;
    readonly type: "edge.observed";
    readonly edge: AgentObservedEdge;
  }) => MaybePromise<void>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/observability.types.ts#L32)

## AgentObservedEdge (interface)

Directed relationship between two stable descriptors.

**Example**

```ts
const edge: AgentObservedEdge = { relationship: "uses-tool", from: "agent", to: "tool" };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentObservedEdge {
  readonly relationship: AgentEdgeRelationship;
  readonly from: string;
  readonly to: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/observability.types.ts#L23)

## AgentRuntimeHooks (interface)

Optional synchronous and asynchronous relationship observers.

**Example**

```ts
const hooks: AgentRuntimeHooks = { onObservedEdge: console.log };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentRuntimeHooks {
  /** Receives one immutable edge synchronously.
   * @param edge - Observed relationship.
   * @returns Nothing.
   * @example hooks.onObservedEdge?.(edge);
   */
  readonly onObservedEdge?: (edge: AgentObservedEdge) => void;
  readonly observability?: AgentObservabilitySink;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/observability.types.ts#L49)

## `runtime-native-events.ts`

---

## Exports Grouped by Category

- [utils](#utils)
  - [collectNativeEvents](#collectnativeevents)
  - [collectNativeEventsEffect](#collectnativeeventseffect)

---

# utils

## collectNativeEvents

Collects native events for existing Promise runtime callers.

**Throws**

The original stream, sink, validation, or cancellation error.

**Example**

```ts
await collectNativeEvents(
  events,
  sink,
  ids,
  names,
  signal,
  limits,
  schemas,
  failure,
  observe,
  abort,
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const collectNativeEvents: (
  events: AsyncIterable<ProtocolEvent>,
  sink: AgentContentSink | undefined,
  toolIds: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
  relkitNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  limits: { readonly maxSteps: number; readonly maxToolCalls: number },
  stateSchemas: ReadonlyMap<string, StandardSchemaV1>,
  failure: () => unknown,
  observe: ((event: ProtocolEvent) => void) | undefined,
  abort: (reason: unknown) => void,
  eventSchemas?: Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1>>,
) => Promise<void>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/runtime-native-events.ts#L70)

## collectNativeEventsEffect

Collects native events with linked Effect interruption.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(
  collectNativeEventsEffect(
    events,
    sink,
    ids,
    names,
    signal,
    limits,
    schemas,
    failure,
    observe,
    abort,
  ),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const collectNativeEventsEffect: (
  events: AsyncIterable<ProtocolEvent>,
  sink: AgentContentSink | undefined,
  toolIds: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
  relkitNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  limits: { readonly maxSteps: number; readonly maxToolCalls: number },
  stateSchemas: ReadonlyMap<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>,
  failure: () => unknown,
  observe: ((event: ProtocolEvent) => void) | undefined,
  abort: (reason: unknown) => void,
  eventSchemas?: Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>> | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<undefined, AgentInvocationFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/runtime-native-events.ts#L29)

## AgentContentSink (interface)

Sends agent output and events to the surrounding runtime.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface AgentContentSink {
  readonly emitOutput: (value: unknown, signal: AbortSignal) => MaybePromise<void>;
  readonly finishOutput?: (signal: AbortSignal) => MaybePromise<void>;
  readonly progressSinkForTool?: (tool: {
    readonly toolCallId: string;
    readonly toolId: string;
  }) => import("@relkit/invocation").ProgressSink;
  readonly emitTool?: (
    value: {
      readonly toolCallId: string;
      readonly toolId: string;
      readonly state: import("./state.types.js").ToolPartState;
      readonly value?: unknown;
    },
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ) => MaybePromise<void>;
  readonly emitEvent?: (
    value: import("./runtime-events.js").AgentExecutionEvent,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ) => MaybePromise<void>;
  readonly emitMessage?: (
    value: import("@relkit/contracts").BrowserMessage,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ) => MaybePromise<void>;
  readonly emitWaiting?: (
    value: Omit<import("./state.types.js").AgentWaitingState, "revision" | "runId">,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ) => MaybePromise<void>;
}
```

## "./signal.types.js" (namespace export)

Re-exports all named exports from the "./signal.types.js" module.

**Signature**

```ts
export * from "./signal.types.js";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L10)

## ExecutionSignalClock (class)

Replaceable clock and timer boundary for execution deadlines.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.provide(createExecutionSignalEffect(options), ExecutionSignalClockLive);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class ExecutionSignalClock
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L15)

## ExecutionSignalClockLive

Live wall clock and timer implementation.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(Effect.provide(createExecutionSignalEffect(options), ExecutionSignalClockLive));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const ExecutionSignalClockLive: Layer.Layer<ExecutionSignalClock, never, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L22)

## acquireExecutionSignalEffect

Acquires an execution signal whose owner closes it with its Effect scope.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.scoped(
  Effect.gen(function* () {
    const active = yield* acquireExecutionSignalEffect(options);
  }),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const acquireExecutionSignalEffect: (
  options: ExecutionSignalOptions,
) => Effect.Effect<ExecutionSignalHandle, AgentInvocationFailure, ExecutionSignalClock | Scope>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L105)

## createExecutionSignal

Creates a live signal for existing callers that own and close the handle.

**Throws**

The original invalid deadline error.

**Example**

```ts
const active = createExecutionSignal(options);
try {
  await work(active.signal);
} finally {
  active.close();
}
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createExecutionSignal: (options: ExecutionSignalOptions) => ExecutionSignalHandle;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L115)

## createExecutionSignalEffect

Creates a live execution signal with an owned timer and caller listener.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(Effect.provide(createExecutionSignalEffect(options), testClock));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createExecutionSignalEffect: (
  options: ExecutionSignalOptions,
) => Effect.Effect<ExecutionSignalHandle, AgentInvocationFailure, ExecutionSignalClock>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L53)

## signalFailure

Converts an abort reason for existing synchronous callers.

**Example**

```ts
signalFailure(signal);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const signalFailure: (signal: AbortSignal) => AgentRuntimeError;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L44)

## signalFailureEffect

Converts an abort reason to a safe invocation error.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(signalFailureEffect(signal));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const signalFailureEffect: (
  signal: AbortSignal,
) => Effect.Effect<AgentRuntimeError, never, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L33)

## withSignal

Races work with a caller signal for existing Promise callers.

**Throws**

The original work error or AgentRuntimeError on cancellation.

**Example**

```ts
await withSignal(fetchData(), signal);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const withSignal: <T>(work: MaybePromise<T>, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L144)

## withSignalEffect

Races Promise work with caller and Effect cancellation.

**Example**

```ts
await Effect.runPromise(withSignalEffect(work, signal));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const withSignalEffect: <T>(
  work: MaybePromise<T>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  linkEffectSignal?: boolean | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<T, AgentInvocationFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.ts#L129)

---

# utils

## ExecutionSignalClockService (interface)

Replaceable deadline timer and current time boundary.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ExecutionSignalClockService {
  /** Reads the current wall time in milliseconds.
   * @returns Epoch milliseconds.
   * @example clock.now();
   */
  readonly now: () => number;
  /** Schedules a deadline callback.
   * @param callback - Deadline action.
   * @param delayMs - Nonnegative delay.
   * @returns Opaque timer handle.
   * @example clock.setTimeout(onDeadline, 1000);
   */
  readonly setTimeout: (callback: () => void, delayMs: number) => unknown;
  /** Cancels a scheduled callback.
   * @param handle - Opaque timer handle.
   * @returns Nothing.
   * @example clock.clearTimeout(handle);
   */
  readonly clearTimeout: (handle: unknown) => void;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.types.ts#L17)

## ExecutionSignalHandle (interface)

Live execution signal whose owner must call close once finished.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ExecutionSignalHandle {
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  /** Clears the deadline timer and caller signal listener.
   * @returns Nothing; repeated calls are safe.
   * @example handle.close();
   */
  readonly close: () => void;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.types.ts#L7)

## ExecutionSignalOptions (type alias)

Options whose limits and caller signal determine an execution deadline.

**Signature**

```ts
type ExecutionSignalOptions = AgentRuntimeOptions & AgentInvocationOptions;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/signal.types.ts#L4)

```ts
export interface AgentStateProvider {

  claimRun(request: ClaimRunRequest): Promise<ExecutionClaim>;
  renewRunClaim(request: RenewRunClaimRequest): Promise<ExecutionClaim>;
  appendJournal(request: AppendJournalRequest): Promise<JournalReceipt>;
  completeRun(request: CompleteRunRequest): Promise<CompletionReceipt>;
  suspendRun(request: SuspendRunRequest): Promise<SuspensionReceipt>;
  interruptOwnedRun(request: InterruptOwnedRunRequest): Promise<InterruptionReceipt>;
  acceptControl(request: AcceptControlRequest): Promise<ControlReceipt>;
  lookupControlReceipt(request: LookupControlReceiptRequest): Promise<ControlReceiptLookup>;
  readControls(request: ReadControlsRequest): Promise<ControlPage>;
  waitForControls(request: WaitForControlsRequest): Promise<void>;

  settleControl(request: SettleControlRequest): Promise<ControlReceipt>;
  recoverAbandonedControl(request: RecoverAbandonedControlRequest): Promise<ControlReceipt>;
  admitContinuation(request: AdmitContinuationRequest): Promise<ContinuationReceipt>;
  lookupContinuationReceipt(
    request: LookupContinuationReceiptRequest,
  ): Promise<ContinuationReceiptLookup>;
}

```

## decideThreadTransition

Synchronously decides a thread action for existing callers.

**Example**

```ts
const result = decideThreadTransition("idle", "send");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const decideThreadTransition: (
  status: ThreadStatus,
  action: ThreadAction,
) => ThreadTransition;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/thread-transition.ts#L80)

## decideThreadTransitionEffect

Decides a thread action inside a traced Effect and counts the decision.

**Example**

```ts
const result = Effect.runSync(decideThreadTransitionEffect("idle", "send"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const decideThreadTransitionEffect: (
  status: ThreadStatus,
  action: ThreadAction,
) => Effect.Effect<ThreadTransition, never, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/thread-transition.ts#L27)

---

# utils

## AgentTransitionError (type alias)

Stable rejection codes returned for invalid thread actions.

**Signature**

```ts
type AgentTransitionError =
  | "AGENT_BUSY"
  | "AGENT_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"
  | "AGENT_STOPPING"
  | "AGENT_WORKER_INTERRUPTED"
  | "AGENT_INVALID_CONTROL";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/thread-transition.types.ts#L7)

## ThreadAction (type alias)

Actions that a caller may request for an agent thread.

**Signature**

```ts
type ThreadAction = "send" | "steer" | "follow-up" | "stop" | "approve" | "deny";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/thread-transition.types.ts#L4)

## ThreadTransition (type alias)

The accepted next state or the reason the current state cannot advance.

**Signature**

```ts
type ThreadTransition =
  | { readonly accepted: true; readonly nextStatus: ThreadStatus; readonly idempotent: boolean }
  | {
      readonly accepted: false;
      readonly status: ThreadStatus;
      readonly code: AgentTransitionError;
    };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/agents/src/thread-transition.types.ts#L15)
